### [37] [From Critic to Confidence: PPO for Language-Based Quantitative Prediction with Confidence Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12687)
*Mehak Dhaliwal,Rasta Tadayon,Andong Hua,Haewon Jeong,Yao Qin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出CARE-PPO框架，通过强化学习联合学习语言定量预测与置信度估计，在医疗和金融任务上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在语言定量预测中容易产生幻觉和过度自信的错误，需要可靠的不确定性估计来判断何时可以信任模型预测。

Method: 在actor-critic PPO微调中引入置信度对齐奖励函数（基于预测误差），使critic学习与预测质量对齐的价值函数，推理时重用作置信度估计器。

Result: 在医疗和金融两个真实任务、Qwen-3 4B和8B两种规模上，定量预测性能优秀，置信度估计显著优于基于logits和口头化的基线方法，在分布外场景下保持优势，并减少任务特定过拟合。

Conclusion: CARE-PPO通过RL微调实现了预测精度与置信度可靠性的联合提升，泛化性优于监督微调方法。

Abstract: LLMs can perform language-based quantitative prediction from unstructured inputs, but remain susceptible to hallucinations and overconfident errors, making it critical to know not only what a model predicts, but when its predictions can be trusted. We introduce CARE-PPO, a reinforcement learning framework that establishes a connection between loss prediction for uncertainty estimation and actor-critic PPO fine-tuning, enabling joint learning of accurate numerical estimates and reliable confidence signals in language-based quantitative prediction. CARE-PPO uses a Confidence-Aligned Reward for Estimation, defined as a function of prediction error, to provide dense error-aware feedback to the actor while inducing the critic to learn a value function aligned with prediction quality. During inference, we repurpose the critic as a confidence estimator. Across two real-world tasks in healthcare and finance and two Qwen-3 model scales (4B and 8B), CARE-PPO achieves strong quantitative prediction performance, while producing significantly better-aligned confidence estimates through the critic than logit-based and verbalized baselines. These gains persist under realistic out-of-distribution settings across domains, spanning linguistic and domain shifts. Finally, CARE-PPO reduces task-specific overfitting on general instruction-following prompts, consistent with the broader generalization advantages of RL fine-tuning over supervised approaches.

</details>


### [38] [Less Experts, Faster Decoding: Cost-Aware Speculative Decoding for Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12696)
*Jincheng Xie,Runheng Liu,Heyan Huang,Yawen Ling,Hanbin Dai,Yu Zheng,Wen Hu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出EcoSpec，一种面向大规模稀疏MoE模型的成本感知推测解码框架，通过预测并考虑专家激活成本来选择草稿令牌路径，在保持高接受概率的同时减少专家权重内存流量，实现最高1.62倍的端到端解码加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大规模MoE模型中，现有的推测解码草稿选择策略主要优化接受概率，忽视了高概率草稿令牌可能路由到不同专家（专家散射），导致专家权重内存流量增加，降低推测加速的实际收益。

Method: 提出EcoSpec框架，包含三个关键组件：1）轻量级专家预测器，预测每个草稿令牌可能激活的专家；2）动态专家缓冲区，维护当前验证集已覆盖的专家；3）成本感知的草稿树选择算法，在保持高接受概率的同时优先重用已激活专家。该方法不修改目标模型的验证规则。

Result: 在DeepSeek-V3.1 (671B)、Qwen3-235B-A22B和GPT-OSS-120B三个大规模MoE模型上，覆盖推理、编程、问答和对话基准测试，一致地减少了活跃专家足迹，最高实现1.62倍端到端解码加速。

Conclusion: 在MoE模型的推测解码中纳入专家激活成本是提升效率的关键，EcoSpec通过成本感知的草稿选择有效平衡了接受概率和专家重用，为大规模MoE模型的高效推理提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models have become an important approach for scaling Large Language Models (LLMs), but their inference efficiency depends strongly on expert activation patterns. Speculative decoding (SD) accelerates autoregressive generation by verifying multiple draft tokens in parallel, yet existing draft selection strategies primarily optimize acceptance likelihood. In large-scale MoE models, however, selecting draft tokens also determines the union of experts activated during verification. We observe that confidence-driven SD can introduce \textit{expert scattering}: high-probability draft tokens may route to disjoint experts, increasing expert-weight memory traffic and reducing the speedup from speculation. Motivated by this observation, we revisit draft-tree selection under the non-uniform memory-cost structure of MoE inference. We propose \textsc{EcoSpec}, a cost-aware speculative decoding framework that incorporates predicted marginal expert activation cost into draft selection. With a lightweight expert predictor and a dynamic expert buffer, \textsc{EcoSpec} favors draft paths that preserve high acceptance likelihood while reusing experts already covered by the current verification set, without modifying the target-model verification rule. We evaluate \textsc{EcoSpec} on three large-scale MoE models, including DeepSeek-V3.1 (671B), Qwen3-235B-A22B, and GPT-OSS-120B, across reasoning, coding, question-answering, and dialogue benchmarks. \textsc{EcoSpec} consistently reduces active expert footprints and improves end-to-end decoding speed, achieving up to $1.62\times$ speedup. These results show that accounting for expert activation cost is important for efficient speculative decoding in large-scale MoE models.

</details>


### [39] [Epistemic Stance Flexibility Probing: Measuring Prompt-Conditioned Register Shift in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12739)
*Binwen Liu,Yilin Ren*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出ESFP基准测试，通过对比外部归因和自归因提示，评估语言模型在争议性主张上的认知灵活性——即从客观归因到主观立场表达的切换能力。测试发现该能力与模型通用能力正交，且立场内容密度是最强信号指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准（准确率、指令遵循、安全性）无法评估语言模型在争议性主张上区分"专家观点"与"自身观点"两种请求的认知灵活性和响应一致性。

Method: 构建ESFP基准，包含104个精心控制的测试项，覆盖6个认知类别和5个措辞模板。从四个维度评估：词汇自我归因、表征级角色框架响应、句子级立场内容密度（LLM评审团）、跨条件立场一致性。在8个前沿模型（5个供应商）上测试。

Result: 认知灵活性与通用模型能力正交：27B开源模型媲美最强闭源系统；某旗舰模型弱于其轻量版本；推理优化模型未见显著优势。立场内容密度提供最强信号；表面词汇标记（如"我认为"）变化可能不伴随实际立场变化。

Conclusion: ESFP衡量的是模型在不同归因条件下调整认知立场的倾向，而非通用能力评估。提供了逐项自举置信区间、权重敏感性分析及综合评分解释限制。

Abstract: A language model may be asked either what experts believe about a contested claim or what it believes about the claim itself. A trustworthy conversational agent should distinguish these two requests and respond in different epistemic registers: neutral attribution in the first case and stance expression in the second. Whether such a shift occurs-and whether it occurs coherently-is not directly assessed by existing benchmarks for accuracy, instruction following, or safety. We introduce ESFP, a behavioral benchmark that treats the contrast between externally attributed and self-attributed prompts as the fundamental unit of measurement. ESFP consists of 104 carefully controlled items spanning six epistemic categories and five phrasing templates, and evaluates model responses along four complementary dimensions: lexical self-attribution, representation-level responsiveness to role framing, sentence-level stance content density assessed by an LLM judge panel, and cross-condition stance consistency. Evaluating eight frontier models from five vendors, we find that epistemic flexibility is largely orthogonal to general model capability: a 27B open-weight model matches the strongest proprietary systems, the flagship model of one family underperforms its lightweight counterpart, and reasoning-optimized models do not consistently exhibit higher flexibility. Stance content density provides the strongest signal, while surface-level lexical markers such as 'I think' can change substantially without corresponding changes in expressed stance. We provide item-level bootstrap confidence intervals, weight-sensitivity analyses, and an explicit discussion of the interpretation limits of the composite score. ESFP measures a model's propensity to adapt its epistemic stance under changing attribution conditions, rather than a general competence measure.

</details>


### [40] [The One-Word Census: Answer-Choice Conformity Across 44 Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12796)
*Tapan Parikh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 通过"一词普查"（One-Word Census）这一极简工具，研究发现44个语言模型在开放选择任务中表现出高度趋同（例如"任意选一个词"时41%选serendipity），但不同模型的从众程度差异超4倍，且社区微调模型最多样化，旗舰模型最合群；家系内代际趋同递增但最新旗舰出现反转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当语言模型面临大量同等有效选项时，它们会如何选择？是否会趋向于相同的答案？现有研究已充分证明模型间存在趋同现象，但缺乏一个简洁、低成本的系统化测量工具来量化这种趋同的结构和变化。

Method: 设计31个单轮提示，每个提示命名一个有大量有效答案的类别（如"说一种树"），对每个模型重复4次，无系统提示。以精确匹配标准化token的方式进行答案分析（无嵌入、无评判），每个模型成本约1美元。使用留一法计算答案选择惊奇度（answer-choice surprisal），即模型答案在其他所有模型答案分布下的平均负对数概率。

Result: 在31个类别中有7个类别的单一答案占比超80%；模型间的从众程度差异超过4倍，persona调优和社区调优的模型最分散，最新旗舰模型最合群（几乎不产生其他模型未给出的答案）。Claude、GPT、Qwen、Grok四个家系内代际趋同递增，但Claude和GPT的最新旗舰出现反转。跨家系留一法的排名鲁棒性rho=0.985。与人类类别产出规范相比，模型在20个共享类别中的18个上比人类更集中。

Conclusion: "一词普查"作为一个极简工具，能够有效揭示语言模型在开放生成任务中的趋同结构。模型趋同程度存在系统性的结构化差异：社区微调模型更多样、旗舰模型更合群，且代际趋势在头部模型中出现反转信号，可能反映了顶级模型定位的调整。

Abstract: When a language model must pick one answer from a large space of equally valid options, which does it pick -- and how often is it the same answer every other model picks? Asked to "pick a word -- any word," 44 models chose "serendipity" 41% of the time. We characterize this convergence with a deliberately minimal instrument: 31 single-turn prompts, each naming a category with many valid one-word answers ("Name a tree."), asked four times per model with no system prompt. Analysis is exact-match on normalized tokens -- no embeddings, no judge -- at about a dollar per model. That models converge is well documented; our contribution is the instrument itself -- the One-Word Census -- and what it reveals about the structure of the convergence. We score each model by answer-choice surprisal: the average $-\log2$ probability of its answers under the pooled answers of all other models, leave-one-out. Convergence is extreme -- in 7 of 31 categories one answer takes over 80% of all answers -- yet conformity varies more than fourfold across models, and the variation is structured. Persona- and community-tuned models are the most divergent; the newest mainline flagships are the most conformist, producing almost no answer no other model gave. Within four lineages (Claude, GPT, Qwen, Grok) conformity rises with each generation -- but reverses for the latest flagship Claude and GPT models, a possible early signal of repositioning at the top tier. Rankings are robust to roster composition (leave-one-family-out rho = 0.985). Against human category-production norms, the field is more concentrated than people in 18 of 20 shared categories. All prompts, transcripts, and code are public.

</details>


### [41] [Knowledgeless Language Models: Suppressing Parametric Recall for Evidence-Grounded Language Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12831)
*Roi Cohen,Yvan Carré,Nick Lechtenbörger,Hendrik Droste,Lucas Kerschke,Russa Biswas,Gerard de Melo,Jan Buys*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出知识缺失语言模型（KLLMs），通过在预训练时对命名实体进行匿名化处理，减少模型对参数化知识的依赖，使其更倾向于基于给定证据进行推理。实验表明，KLLMs在上下文问答、事实验证和幻觉检测任务上优于标准语言模型，在不完美证据的检索增强场景中相对提升20-25%，且具有更好的校准性和弃权行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型在参数中编码了大量事实知识，当这些知识过时、不完整或与上下文不一致时，会导致不可靠行为。本文旨在探索是否可以通过修改预训练信号，系统性地使模型从参数化记忆转向基于证据的推理。

Method: 提出知识缺失语言模型（KLLMs），在预训练语料中对命名实体进行匿名化处理，移除实体链接的事实监督信号，从而减少模型在预训练阶段获取实体相关的参数化知识。

Result: KLLMs在多个规模下一致优于基线模型：在上下文问答、事实验证和幻觉检测任务上表现更好；在不完美检索证据的场景中相对提升20-25%；具有更优的校准性（ECE、Brier分数、AUROC）和更可靠的弃权行为。

Conclusion: 抑制预训练阶段的实体监督可以诱导模型的认知行为转变，使其更少依赖参数化知识、更多依赖外部证据，从而在现实条件下提升可靠性。该方法可作为检索增强和工具系统的补充，提供更倾向于证据感知的基础模型。

Abstract: Language models encode substantial factual knowledge in their parameters, which can lead to unreliable behavior when this knowledge is outdated, incomplete, or misaligned with the provided context. In this work, we study whether modifying the pretraining signal can systematically shift models away from parametric recall and toward evidence-grounded reasoning. We introduce Knowledge--''Less'' Language Models (KLLMs), a fundamentally different epistemic training paradigm for LLMs, which are pretrained on corpora in which named entities are anonymized, thereby removing a primary channel for entity-linked factual supervision. This intervention substantially reduces closed-book factual recall, while often improving performance on tasks where relevant information is provided as context. Across multiple model scales, KLLMs consistently outperform matched baselines on contextual question answering, fact verification, and hallucination detection benchmarks. Crucially, in retrieval-grounded settings with imperfect evidence, KLLMs show improved robustness and achieve up to 20--25\% relative gains over standard language models. They further exhibit better calibration, with improved ECE, Brier score, and AUROC, as well as more reliable abstention behavior. Our results demonstrate that suppressing entity-linked supervision during pretraining induces a shift in epistemic behavior: KLLMs rely less on parametric knowledge and more on external evidence, leading to improved reliability under realistic conditions. This suggests that pretraining-time control over knowledge acquisition can complement retrieval-augmented and tool-based systems by providing a more evidence-sensitive base model.

</details>


### [42] [Can LLMs Write Reliable Rubrics? A Meta-Evaluation for Experiment Reproduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12835)
*Hanhua Hong,Yizhi Li,Jiaoyan Chen,Luu Gia Huy,Sophia Ananiadou,Jung-jae Kim,Chenghua Lin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次对LLM生成的论文复现评估准则（rubrics）进行系统性元评估，发现增强生成设置能显著提升下游评估一致性，接近人类基线水平，但LLM生成的准则存在粒度过细、高分偏向、领域适应性不足等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于准则的评估是评价LLM研究智能体开放输出的有前景方法，尤其在论文复现任务中。但构建论文专属准则需要大量专家投入，限制了基准测试（如PaperBench）的可扩展性。

Method: 将准则重构为清单格式，在两种主干模型上评估四种生成设置。通过内在语义相似度和外在评分对齐度进行元评估。

Result: 增强生成设置显著提升下游评估对齐度，最强设置接近人类基线；内在语义提升较温和。LLM生成的准则通常过于细粒度、偏向高分、对论文领域适应性不足。

Conclusion: LLM生成的准则在自动评估论文复现方面有潜力且能接近人类水平，但仍存在粒度、偏差和领域适应等局限，需进一步改进。

Abstract: Rubric-based evaluation is a promising approach for assessing open-ended outputs from LLM-based research agents, particularly in paper reproduction, where direct paper-to-repository comparison is prone to hallucination. However, constructing paper-specific rubrics requires substantial expert effort, limiting the scalability of benchmarks such as PaperBench. In this work, we present, to our knowledge, the first systematic meta-evaluation of LLM-generated rubrics for paper reproduction. We reformulate rubrics into a checklist-style format and evaluate four generation settings across two backbone models. We meta-evaluate generated rubrics intrinsically by semantic similarity and extrinsically by score alignment with ground-truth rubrics. Our results show that the augmented settings substantially improves downstream evaluation alignment, with the strongest setting approaching the human baseline, while intrinsic gains are more modest. Further analyses reveal that LLM-generated rubrics are often overly fine-grained, biased toward high scores, and less adaptive to paper domains, highlighting both the affordances and limitations.

</details>


### [43] [Evaluating Large Language Models on Misconceptions in Multi-Turn Medical Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12884)
*Monica Munnangi,Saiph Savage*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ThReadMed-QA多轮医疗对话数据集，用于评估LLM在多轮交互中检测和纠正患者错误前提的能力。实验发现，即使用最先进模型，在单轮中约85%的纠错率会在两轮后续交互后骤降至约50%，且错误传播是性能下降的主因，揭示现有评估框架忽视了多轮对话中的安全隐患。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估框架无法捕捉LLM在多轮对话中处理患者错误前提（misconceptions）的行为，而这些错误前提在对话过程中可能产生、持续或演变，但模型能否在长时间对话中可靠地纠正这些误解尚未被充分研究。

Method: 基于AskDocs真实患者交互，构建包含2,437条医患会话线程（8,204个问答对）的ThReadMed-QA多轮对话数据集；采用基于评分标准的LLM-as-a-Judge框架评估五个LLM在检测和纠正错误前提方面的能力；引入oracle分析（将模型先前输出替换为医生回答）以探究错误传播效应。

Result: GPT-5和Claude-Haiku在首轮问题中纠正错误前提约85%，但在两轮追问后跌至约50%；oracle分析表明性能降级主要由错误传播驱动，且在正确上下文下表现仍不完美。

Conclusion: 即使前沿LLM在单轮中能纠正错误前提，其性能在多轮对话中显著下降，导致患者场景下不一致且可能不安全的指导，凸显了构建多轮行为评估框架的必要性。

Abstract: Patients seeking medical information often ask questions that embed incorrect assumptions or misconceptions. In such cases, safe medical communication requires not only answering the question, but identifying and correcting the underlying false belief. These interactions naturally unfold over multiple turns, a pattern now mirrored in interactions with LLMs. Yet current evaluation frameworks do not capture model behavior in these settings, where misconceptions can emerge, persist, or evolve over the course of a conversation. Whether LLMs can reliably correct such misconceptions over time remains largely unexamined. To study this, we introduce ThReadMed-QA, a multi-turn medical dialogue dataset of 2,437 patient-physician conversation threads comprising 8,204 question-answer pairs, derived from real patient interactions on AskDocs. This dataset enables systematic evaluation of whether models can detect and correct misconceptions under a multi-turn context. We evaluate five LLMs using a rubric-based LLM-as-a-Judge framework that scores responses based on their ability to identify and correct misconceptions. Our experiments reveal a consistent pattern: even frontier models that can address misconceptions in a single interaction degrade substantially over subsequent turns. GPT-5 and Claude-Haiku correct these false presuppositions around 85% on initial questions but drop to roughly 50% within two follow-ups. An oracle analysis replacing prior model outputs with physician responses shows that much of the degradation is driven by error propagation, while performance remains imperfect even under correct context. Even when models tend to correct misconceptions initially, their performance degrades substantially over later turns, leading to inconsistent and potentially unsafe guidance in patient-facing settings and highlighting the need for evaluation frameworks that capture multi-turn behavior.

</details>


### [44] [LLM Judges Can Be Too Generous When There Is No Reference Answer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12885)
*Chalamalasetti Kranti,Sowmya Vajjala*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLM裁判在无参考答案评估中的可靠性，发现裁判模型倾向于高估错误答案，而加入参考答案后决策翻转最高达85%，且与人类判断一致，强调了使用前需进行参考感知校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究LLM裁判在无参考答案（no-reference）的开放式评估场景中是否真正可靠，填补当前对该类评估设置可靠性研究的空白。

Method: 采用两阶段流水线：a) 校准实验，评估裁判模型对评估任务的自身知识掌握程度；b) 敏感性实验，考察参考答案的存在与位置对裁判模型性能的影响。实验覆盖三种语言。

Result: LLM裁判在缺乏参考答案时倾向于高估错误答案；加入参考答案后，裁判模型的正确/错误决策翻转幅度在某些设置下高达85%；这些参考驱动的变化与人类判断基本一致。

Conclusion: 在将LLM裁判可靠地用于无参考评估之前，必须通过包含参考感知评估的样本进行校准；本文方法可作为其他任务中LLM裁判校准的蓝图。

Abstract: LLM judges are increasingly being used to evaluate open-ended model responses, often in no-reference settings where a ground-truth answer is unavailable. However, can they reliably assess in such evaluation setups? We explore this question in this paper through a two stage pipeline with a) calibration experiments that assess the judge model's knowledge of the task it is evaluating, and b) sensitivity experiments that assess how the judge model's performance is impacted by the presence and positioning of the reference answer in the prompt. Across experiments covering three languages, we show that the judge models we evaluated tend to over-credit incorrect answers in the absence of a reference answer, and adding reference answer information to the prompt flips the judge model's correct/incorrect decisions by as much as 85% in some experimental settings. Comparison with a subset of human annotations shows that these reference-driven changes generally align with human judgments. Our results emphasize the need for calibrating the LLM judges with a sample with reference-aware evaluation before using them in reference-free setups reliably, and our methodology provides a blueprint for researchers and practitioners in doing such calibration of LLM judges for other tasks.

</details>


### [45] [The Illusion of Robustness: Aggregate Accuracy Hides Prediction Flips under Task-Irrelevant Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12963)
*Yanzhe Zhang,Sanmi Koyejo,Diyi Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 大语言模型在长上下文场景下，整体准确率看似稳健，但实际存在显著的逐样本不稳定性：任务无关的上下文（甚至无意义伪词）会在一小部分样本上双向影响模型预测，且这种影响具有模型特异性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM部署在上下文丰富的场景中，任务输入常伴随长且部分无关的上下文。现有研究多关注聚合层面的鲁棒性，忽略了逐样本层面的不稳定性，本文旨在揭示这种被聚合准确率掩盖的尾部风险。

Method: 在受控设置下，将任务无关上下文（包括语义上无意义的伪词）添加到基准问题前，测量模型在聚合层面和逐样本层面的准确率变化，并分析上下文类型、长度、测试时计算和模型开发阶段等因素的调节作用。

Result: 1) 聚合层面模型看似鲁棒，但逐样本层面存在显著不稳定性；2) 无意义伪词也能在少量样本上显著改变预测，且影响是双向的（部分样本变差，部分变好）；3) 这种两向效应在多种模型和数据集上一致存在，但受影响的样本具有模型特异性；4) 不稳定性受上下文类型、长度、测试时计算和模型开发阶段等因素调节。

Conclusion: 聚合准确率掩盖了上下文引发的尾部风险，需要逐样本层面的可靠性评估来更全面地理解LLM在实际部署中的行为。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) grow more capable, they are increasingly deployed in context-rich settings where task inputs are often accompanied by long, partially irrelevant context. In a controlled setting, we find that state-of-the-art models often appear robust to task-irrelevant context at the aggregate level: prepending it to benchmark questions causes little change in overall accuracy. This aggregate stability, however, masks significant per-example instability. Even semantically meaningless pseudo-words, formed by randomly combining characters, can markedly shift model predictions on a small fraction of examples, degrading performance on some while improving it on others. This two-sided effect holds consistently across a wide range of models and datasets, yet the affected examples are largely model-specific. We further show that this instability is modulated by context type, context length, test-time compute, and model development stage. Together, our findings reveal context-induced tail risks concealed by aggregate accuracy, motivating per-example reliability evaluation of language models.

</details>


### [46] [PalmClaw: A Native On-Device Agent Framework for Mobile Phones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13027)
*Hongru Cai,Yongqi Li,Ran Wei,Wenjie Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PalmClaw是一个原生运行在手机上的开源智能体框架，通过将设备能力封装为带有明确参数、结构化结果和清晰执行边界的设备工具，实现了比最强基线11.5%的任务成功率提升和94.9%的完成时间缩减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有移动智能体主要通过GUI动作（点击、滑动、输入）操作手机，形成长序列、依赖界面的操作链路，无法直接访问设备原生的传感器、数据和应用能力，且执行边界难以界定。

Method: 提出PalmClaw框架，在手机上原生运行，将设备能力（传感器、数据、应用等）封装为带有明确参数、结构化结果和清晰执行边界的设备工具（device tools），智能体直接调用这些工具而非模拟GUI操作。

Result: 相比最强基线方法，任务成功率相对提升11.5%，完成时间减少94.9%，设置负担更低，且有清晰的执行边界痕迹可追踪。

Conclusion: 将设备能力抽象为显式工具的范式比传统的GUI操作方式更高效、可靠，为移动端智能体提供了更可控的执行边界和更好的用户体验。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents have moved beyond generating responses to executing multi-step tasks by calling tools, observing the results, and iteratively deciding the next action. Most agent systems run on desktops or servers, which support tool use and task automation. Mobile devices are also important agent environments because they are widely accessible and contain users' data, sensors, and daily-use applications. Existing mobile agents mainly operate smartphones through graphical user interface (GUI) actions such as tapping, swiping, and typing, which often form long, interface-dependent sequences, cannot directly access device capabilities, and make execution boundaries difficult to define. We present \textbf{PalmClaw}, an open-source agent framework that runs natively on mobile phones and manages the sessions, memory, skills, tools, and agent loop directly on the device. PalmClaw exposes device capabilities as device tools with explicit arguments, structured results, and clearly defined execution boundaries. This design enables agents to use mobile capabilities directly while keeping each action explicit and controlled. Experiments show an 11.5\% relative improvement in task success and a 94.9\% reduction in completion time over the strongest baseline, with lower setup burden and traces illustrating how execution boundaries are applied. Code is available at https://github.com/ModalityDance/PalmClaw.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [47] [Cross-Cutting Security Analysis of LLM-Generated Code via Metamorphic Testing and Association Rule Mining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12089)
*Zedong Peng,Chenggang Wang,Shangyue Zhu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一个结合安全导向的蜕变关系(MRs)与关联规则(AR)挖掘的框架，用于检测LLM生成代码中的安全漏洞、揭示其共违例结构，并将该结构追溯到prompt级别的风险因素。对5个开源模型的3700个代码片段分析发现68.8%违反至少一个MR，且XSS与弱加密共违例以82.5%置信度预测硬编码凭证等跨领域模式，证明不安全代码生成是一种结构化的、受prompt条件影响的现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM生成的代码中安全漏洞普遍存在，但这些漏洞往往跨多个关注领域同时出现，反映了安全的横切性(cross-cutting)本质，而现有研究缺乏对漏洞之间共现结构和其与prompt关系的系统分析。

Method: 定义覆盖SQL注入、XSS、命令注入、路径遍历、硬编码凭证、弱密码学、内存安全错误等主要CWE类别的9种蜕变关系；使用基于LLM的判断器对LLMSecEval基准中的3700个代码片段进行测试；应用关联规则挖掘发现共违例模式；进行prompt级别的风险分析。

Result: 68.8%的代码片段违反至少一个MR；硬编码凭证(79.1%)和命令注入(74.4%)是最普遍的违例类型；XSS与弱加密共违例预测硬编码凭证的置信度为82.5%(提升度3.23)；65.5%的prompt在所有5个模型上产生一致的违例结果。

Conclusion: 不安全代码生成并非独立缺陷的集合，而是一种结构化的、受prompt条件影响的现象，需要聚类感知的验证和prompt级别的干预措施来实现更安全的LLM辅助编程。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) frequently generate code with security vulnerabilities, yet these weaknesses are rarely isolated: they often span multiple concern areas simultaneously, reflecting the cross-cutting nature of security in software. We present a framework that combines security-oriented Metamorphic Relations (MRs) with Association Rule (AR) mining to detect vulnerabilities in LLM-generated code, uncover their co-violation structure, and trace that structure back to prompt-level risk factors. We define nine MRs covering major CWE categories, including SQL injection, XSS, command injection, path traversal, hard-coded credentials, weak cryptography, and memory-safety errors, and apply them using an LLM-based judge to 3,700 code snippets generated by five open models from the LLMSecEval benchmark. The results show that 68.8% of snippets violate at least one MR, with hard-coded credentials (79.1%) and command injection (74.4%) among the most prevalent applicable failures. AR mining reveals strong cross-cutting co-violation patterns, notably that XSS and weak cryptography co-violations predict hard-coded credentials with 82.5% confidence (lift = 3.23), along with tightly coupled clusters linking authentication, credential handling, and cryptographic weakness, as well as input-handling and memory-safety failures. We then perform prompt-level risk analysis and find that database- and authentication-related prompts are strong predictors of broad cross-cutting insecurity, while 65.5% of prompts yield consistent violation outcomes across all five models. These findings show that insecure code generation is not merely a collection of independent defects, but a structured and prompt-conditioned phenomenon, motivating cluster-aware verification and prompt-level intervention for safer LLM-assisted programming.

</details>


### [48] [MindReader: Using LLMs to Encourage Memorable and Secure Password Replacement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12148)
*Anna Gerchanovsky,Lujo Bauer,Michael K. Reiter*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: MindReader利用LLM帮助用户在更换密码时生成语义相关但语法无关的替代密码，在保持可记忆性的同时提升安全性。用户研究表明，即使攻击者知晓原始密码和工具实现，MindReader生成的密码也比其他替换密码更难被在线攻击破解，且记忆性与原始密码相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户在被要求更换密码时，往往会选择原始密码的可预测变体，导致安全性不足。设计MindReader旨在帮助用户生成既易于记忆又难以被攻击者猜测的密码替换方案。

Method: 利用LLM推断原始密码各组成部分的语义含义，然后建议语义相关但语法不相关的新密码组件，从而生成替换密码。

Result: 用户研究中，使用MindReader生成的密码比不使用MindReader的替换密码以及原始密码更安全。即使攻击者知晓原始密码和工具完整实现，MindReader生成的密码在在线攻击中更难被猜中。同时，一周后的登录测试表明其记忆性与替代密码和原始密码相当。

Conclusion: MindReader能够在密码更换场景中有效平衡安全性与可记忆性，为用户提供了一种优于传统自行替换密码的方案。

Abstract: We report on the design and evaluation of MindReader, a tool that helps a user replace her password when she is required to do so. Left to their own devices, users tend to replace their previous passwords with predictable variations of the original ones. MindReader leverages LLMs to suggest password variations that are chosen to be easy for the user to remember but harder for an attacker to predict. To do this, MindReader infers the meaning behind original password components and then suggests semantically related (yet syntactically unrelated) components for the new password. In a user study, passwords created using MindReader were more secure than both replacement passwords created without using MindReader and original passwords. In particular, MindReader replacement passwords were harder to guess in an online attack than alternative replacement passwords even by an attacker with knowledge of the original password and full knowledge of the tool implementation. Passwords created with MindReader were also comparably memorable to alternative replacement passwords and original passwords, as measured by the ability of users to successfully log in a week after creating their password.

</details>


### [49] [MTD-Playground: An Attacker-Aware Evaluation Framework for Network Moving Target Defense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12199)
*Mohammad Farhad,Mohoshin Ara Tahera,Padam Jung Thapa,Shuvalaxmi Dass,Bhupendra Acharya*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出MTD-Playground，一个面向SDN路径随机化(PR)移动目标防御技术的攻击者感知评估框架，在真实企业级多阶段攻击场景下进行基准测试。实验表明，激进变异间隔可将攻击成功率降至4-20%，攻击完成时间增至160-311秒，同时吞吐量提升30.9%，内部路径延迟降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MTD评估因攻击者假设、攻击场景和评估指标不一致而碎片化，缺乏可重复性和面向部署的对比框架。

Method: 提出MTD-Playground评估框架，引入复合评估方法论，从部署效能、变异间隔权衡、攻防操作平衡三个维度综合评估SDN路径随机化MTD技术。

Result: 激进变异间隔将攻击成功率降至4-20%，攻击完成时间增至160-311秒；PR-MTD提升吞吐量30.9%并降低路径延迟；短变异间隔实现最高部署效能和正向防御优势。

Conclusion: SDN-based PR-MTD能在企业环境中有效破坏多阶段攻击进程，同时保持实际可部署性。

Abstract: Moving Target Defense (MTD) has emerged as a proactive network cyber defense paradigm that increases attacker uncertainty through dynamic network reconfiguration techniques such as Software-Defined Networking (SDN)-enabled path randomization. However, existing evaluations remain fragmented due to inconsistent attacker assumptions, attack scenarios, and evaluation metrics, limiting reproducibility and deployment-oriented comparison. In this paper, we present MTD-Playground, an attacker-aware evaluation framework for benchmarking SDN-enabled path-randomization (PR) MTD techniques under realistic enterprise-style multi-stage attack scenarios. Beyond isolated security and performance metrics, MTD-Playground introduces a composite evaluation methodology for analyzing deployment effectiveness, mutation-interval trade-offs, and defender-attacker operational balance. Using periodic path randomization as a representative PR-MTD strategy, our evaluation shows that aggressive mutation intervals reduce attack success rates to 4-20% while increasing attack completion time to 160-311s across evaluated attack scenarios. At the same time, PR-MTD improves throughput by up to 30.9% and reduces internal-path latency without service interruption. Composite analysis further shows that shorter mutation intervals consistently achieve the highest deployment effectiveness and positive defender advantage. These results demonstrate that SDN-based PR-MTD can substantially disrupt multi-stage attack progression while remaining practically deployable in enterprise environments.

</details>


### [50] [Explaining Intrusion Alert Decisions of Deep Learning-based Network Intrusion Detection Systems for Security Analysts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12203)
*Ayush Kumar,Vrizlynn L. L. Thing*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出EXP-SEC框架，用于解释基于深度学习的网络入侵检测系统（DL-based NIDS）的告警决策。该框架包含三个核心模块：取证模块（定位可疑数据包/流）、解释模块（处理复杂特征依赖关系，支持重叠分组和组间重要性差异）、多阶段映射模块（将特征级解释转化为安全分析师可理解的领域特定解释）。实验表明，EXP-SEC在组级和重叠感知的可解释性指标上优于现有最佳框架xNIDS，在传统特征级指标上表现相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DL-based NIDS的解释方法未能与安全运营中心（SOC）分析师的专业知识对齐，缺乏对网络流量中复杂特征依赖关系（如特征可分重叠组且组间重要性不同）的处理能力，且无法将技术性解释转化为分析师可理解的领域特定格式。

Method: EXP-SEC框架包含三个模块：(1) 取证模块：隔离触发告警的可疑数据包/流；(2) 解释模块：处理重叠特征分组及组间重要性差异的复杂依赖关系；(3) 多阶段映射模块：将特征/组级解释转化为领域特定的解释，供安全分析师处理。

Result: 在组级和重叠感知的可解释性指标上，EXP-SEC优于现有最佳框架xNIDS；在传统特征级指标（描述准确性、稀疏性、稳定性）上与xNIDS表现相当；能够生成安全分析师友好的解释格式。

Conclusion: EXP-SEC框架成功将DL-based NIDS的入侵检测决策解释与SOC分析师的专业知识对齐，通过处理重叠特征组依赖和生成领域特定解释，显著提升了可解释性的实用性。

Abstract: In this paper, we present EXP-SEC, a novel framework which can explain the intrusion detection decisions of DL-based NIDS (which lead to security alerts) in a way that is aligned with the domain knowledge of analysts working in Security Operations Center (SOC). We highlight the following features of our framework: (1) a forensic module that isolates the suspect packets/flow which likely caused an alert (2) an explanation module which can handle much more complex feature dependencies in network traffic than existing methods (features can be divided into overlapping groups and some groups are more important than others), and (3) a multi-stage mapping module which translates the feature/group-based explanations generated by explanation module to domain-specific explanations suitable for processing by security analysts. We evaluate EXP-SEC with state-of-the-art DL-based NIDS and our evaluation results show that EXP-SEC outperforms xNIDS (existing best performing explanation framework) in terms of group-level and overlap-aware explanation utility metrics while performing similarly in terms of conventional feature-level metrics such as descriptive accuracy, sparsity and stability. Moreover, taking the case of a state-of-the-art DL-based NIDS, we demonstrate the security analyst-friendly explanation format generated by EXP-SEC.

</details>


### [51] [$\mathrm{P}^{3}$CDA: Privacy-Preserving and Provably Secure Cross Domain Authentication Scheme for Internet of Drones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12288)
*Chengqi Hou,Beibei Li,Ziqing Zhu,Yang You,Licheng Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出P³CDA，一种面向IoD环境的隐私保护且可证明安全的跨域认证方案，通过高效假名管理机制、结构增强的Merkle Hash Tree和基于密码累加器的跨域认证协议，在CK adversary模型下实现安全、高效且支持身份隐私保护的跨域认证，实验表明其计算、通信和存储开销均优于现有方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IoD环境下跨域交互日益频繁，现有认证方案难以同时满足强安全性、高效率和身份隐私保护，无法适应高动态、资源受限的IoD场景需求。

Method: 1) 设计高效假名管理机制，支持自适应假名生成及批量注册、验证和撤销；2) 提出结构增强的Merkle Hash Tree，支持批量假名更新，降低存储开销；3) 基于密码累加器构建跨域认证协议，支持匿名认证、可追溯性和恶意无人机高效撤销。

Result: 在CK adversary模型下完成了严格安全分析和形式化安全证明，大量实验表明P³CDA在计算、通信和存储开销方面均优于现有方案。

Conclusion: P³CDA有效平衡了安全性、效率和隐私保护，适用于高动态且资源受限的IoD跨域认证场景。

Abstract: With the rapid expansion of the Internet of Drones (IoD) and the increasing mobility of drones, cross-domain interactions among geographically distributed domains have become inevitable. Cross-domain authentication is therefore a fundamental security requirement for IoD. However, existing authentication schemes often struggle to simultaneously achieve strong security, high efficiency, and identity privacy, making them unsuitable for the stringent requirements of highly dynamic and resource-constrained IoD environments. To address this challenge, we propose $\mathrm{P}^{3}$CDA, a privacy-preserving and provably secure cross-domain authentication scheme. First, we design an efficient pseudonym management mechanism that supports adaptive pseudonym generation as well as batch registration, verification, and revocation. Second, we propose a structurally enhanced Merkle Hash Tree (MHT) that supports batch pseudonym updates, thereby reducing the pseudonym storage overhead of drones. Building on these components, we develop a cryptographic accumulator-based cross-domain authentication protocol that enables anonymous authentication with authorized pseudonyms while preserving the traceability and efficient revocation of malicious drones. We rigorously analyze the security of $\mathrm{P}^{3}$CDA and formally prove its security under the Canetti--Krawczyk (CK) adversary model. Extensive experiments demonstrate that $\mathrm{P}^{3}$CDA achieves lower computational, communication, and storage overhead than state-of-the-art schemes.

</details>


### [52] [Antiproof: Synthesizing Vulnerability Detectors and Proofs of Exploitability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12316)
*Alon Shakevsky,Corban Villa,Ion Stoica,Raluca Ada Popa*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Antiproof是一个结合神经符号检测器合成（高召回）与可利用性证明预言机（自动验证）的端到端漏洞发现系统。在BountyBench和KEVBench基准测试中，Antiproof检测到66个漏洞中的64个，召回率提升超过60个百分点；在50个广泛部署系统的扫描中发现了数百个先前未知的漏洞，已获得12个CVE编号，包括Ray、SGLang、vLLM和LiteLLM中的远程代码执行漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有漏洞发现方法在高召回率与可靠自动验证之间存在权衡，且难以在不产生高昂成本的情况下同时实现两者。

Method: Antiproof采用端到端架构：(1) 神经符号检测器合成——从漏洞数据集中学习并迭代精化静态检测器，实现高召回发现；(2) 可利用性证明预言机——通过验证可执行证明是否展示具体攻击者能力来自动验证候选漏洞。

Result: 在BountyBench和KEVBench上检测到66个漏洞中的64个，召回率比静态分析和神经符号基线提升超过60个百分点。在50个广泛部署系统中扫描发现数百个先前未知漏洞，已获12个CVE分配，包括Ray、SGLang、vLLM和LiteLLM中的远程代码执行漏洞。

Conclusion: Antiproof有效地结合了高召回发现与自动验证，能够在大规模系统中发现真实世界的高危零日漏洞，包括可被攻击者用于接管LLM训练和推理系统的RCE漏洞。

Abstract: Discovering vulnerabilities before attackers exploit them requires high recall and reliable automatic validation, but existing approaches struggle to achieve both without prohibitive cost. We present Antiproof, an end-to-end vulnerability discovery system that combines neuro-symbolic detector synthesis for high-recall discovery with proof-of-exploitability oracles for automatic validation. Antiproof learns and iteratively refines static detectors from vulnerability datasets, then validates candidates by verifying whether executable proofs demonstrate concrete attacker capabilities. Evaluated on BountyBench and our curated KEVBench dataset, Antiproof detects 64 of 66 vulnerabilities, improving recall by more than 60 percentage points over static-analysis and neuro-symbolic baselines. In a scan of 50 widely deployed systems, Antiproof uncovered several hundred previously unknown vulnerabilities. We are responsibly disclosing all confirmed zero-days and have received 12 CVE assignments to date, including remote code execution vulnerabilities in Ray, SGLang, vLLM, and LiteLLM that could allow attackers to take over LLM training and inference systems.

</details>


### [53] [Trust but Verify? Uncovering the Security Debt of Autonomous Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12428)
*A H M Nazmus Sakib,Dipayan Banik,Murtuza Jadliwala*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过对AIDev数据集中16,112个文件变更和4,022个拉取请求的大规模实证研究，发现38.9%的AI智能体生成的PR存在安全代码异味，其中供应链完整性问题占82.3%，硬编码凭据占严重安全问题的99.6%。值得注意的是，人类协作者引入了67.6%的真实泄露密钥，而现有自动化及人工审查流程未能检测到81.1%的凭据泄露。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主编码智能体的广泛采用加速了软件开发，但也带来了传统人工审查难以跟上的安全风险。以往研究主要关注这些系统的功能正确性和生产力，而缺乏对其安全风险的系统性大规模实证研究。

Method: 使用AIDev数据集，结合经过验证的LLM-as-a-judge框架和人工定性分析，对4,022个拉取请求中的16,112个文件变更进行安全代码异味的识别与分类。

Result: 38.9%的智能体生成的PR含有安全代码异味；供应链完整性问题占所有检测到的安全异味的82.3%；硬编码凭据占严重安全异味的99.6%；人类协作者引入了67.6%的真实泄露密钥；现有审查流程未能检测到81.1%的凭据泄露。

Conclusion: 智能体辅助的软件开发工作流存在显著的安全风险，可能导致开发者警惕性降低。亟需在人类-AI协作点直接实施上下文感知的安全防护措施。

Abstract: The increasing adoption of autonomous coding agents accelerates software development but also introduces scoped security risks within high-impact file paths that can outpace traditional human review capacity. While prior research has primarily evaluated these systems in terms of functional correctness and productivity, this paper presents a large-scale empirical study using the AIDev dataset to systematically characterize security code smells in agent-generated pull requests (PRs). Through a combination of a validated LLM-as-a-judge framework and manual qualitative analysis, we identify and classify security misconfigurations across 16,112 file changes spanning 4,022 pull requests. Our results reveal that 38.9% of agent-generated PRs contain at least one security smell, with supply chain integrity issues accounting for 82.3% of all detected security smells. Furthermore, hard-coded credentials constitute 99.6% of all critical-severity security smells. Crucially, we find that human collaborators are responsible for introducing 67.6% of genuine leaked secrets within these agent-assisted workflows, while existing automated and human review processes fail to detect 81.1% of these credentials prior to integration. These findings highlight substantial security risks in agent-assisted software development workflows and suggest a potential reduction in developer vigilance. They also underscore the urgent need for context-aware security guardrails implemented directly at the point of human-AI collaboration.

</details>


### [54] [On the Security Implications of PQC in TLS: Handshake Exhaustion and IDS Degradation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12504)
*Lin-Fa Lee,Yi-Yu Chang,Chia-Mu Yu,Kuo-Hui Yeh*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本研究构建了包含PQC-TLS服务器和10个攻击节点的实验环境，生成超过16.5GB混合流量数据，发现PQC-TLS可延长服务器高CPU利用率持续时间达88倍，显著放大握手耗尽攻击效果；同时评估了现有深度学习IDS在PQC环境下的检测性能严重下降（Exosphere召回率仅约50%，HyperVision的AU-ROC降至0.49），并开源了数据集和实验代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PQC正被集成到TLS 1.3中，但PQC原语在握手阶段引入的额外计算和通信开销可能放大TLS握手耗尽攻击的影响，导致更严重的DDoS威胁，而现有IDS在PQC环境下的检测能力尚不清楚。

Method: 搭建一个PQC-TLS服务器和10个攻击节点的实验平台，生成16.5GB混合流量（含正常浏览和握手耗尽攻击），通过服务器CPU监控和深度学习IDS评估进行实证分析。

Result: PQC-TLS使服务器高CPU利用率持续时间延长最多88倍；Exosphere召回率仅约50%，HyperVision的AU-ROC降至0.49（接近随机水平），现有IDS在PQC环境下存在严重检测盲区。

Conclusion: 系统量化了PQC环境下IDS检测盲区的根本原因，发布了首个PQC-DDoS混合流量数据集（含精确攻击时间戳和资源监控数据），开源了全部代码和AWS部署脚本，支持可复现的云测试环境，以推动下一代PQC感知入侵检测系统的发展。

Abstract: Post-Quantum Cryptography (PQC) is increasingly being integrated into TLS 1.3 to enhance resilience against quantum-enabled attacks. However, the additional computational and communication overhead introduced by PQC primitives during the handshake phase may also amplify the impact of TLS handshake exhaustion attacks, leading to more severe Distributed Denial-of-Service (DDoS) threats.
  In this study, we establish an empirical testbed consisting of one PQC-enabled TLS server and ten attacking nodes, generating over 16.5 GB of mixed traffic data that includes both legitimate browsing behavior and high-intensity handshake exhaustion attacks.
  Experimental results show that PQC-TLS can prolong periods of sustained high CPU utilization on the server by up to 88 times, significantly amplifying the effectiveness of such attacks. Furthermore, we evaluate state-of-the-art deep learning-based Intrusion Detection Systems (IDS) and observe a substantial decline in attack detection performance under PQC traffic conditions.
  In particular, exosphere achieves only around 50% recall, while HyperVision's AU-ROC degrades to near-random levels (0.49), revealing critical detection blind spots in existing IDS when operating in PQC environments.
  The main contributions of this work are threefold: (1) we systematically quantify and analyze the root causes of IDS detection blind spots in PQC settings; (2) we publicly release a comprehensive PQC-DDoS hybrid traffic dataset, including precise attack timestamps and server-side resource monitoring data; and (3) we open-source all experimental code and AWS deployment scripts, enabling a fully reproducible cloud-based testing environment.
  These resources aim to support both academia and industry in developing next-generation PQC-aware intrusion detection systems.

</details>


### [55] [When Binaries Talk Back: Representation-Confusion Attacks on LLM-Assisted Reverse Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12507)
*Igor Santos-Grueiro*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出逆向工程中的表征混淆攻击（RARE），即攻击者通过构造恶意二进制文件，使提取的数据被错误地赋予指令权威或证据地位。作者构建了RARE-Bench基准测试，并提出RARE-Guard防御框架，通过工具授权、支持门和来源门等机制，将二进制衍生内容限制为数据而非权威证据，有效阻止了虚假主张的验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM辅助逆向工程系统在分析攻击者控制的二进制文件时，会将字符串、反编译输出和工具报告中的内容直接提升为指令、证据或可信分析状态，而二进制文件可以精心构造数据使其看起来像指令或使不同来源的记录看起来像独立证据，这种"表征混淆"会导致系统被误导并采纳攻击者植入的恶意操作。

Method: 1）构建RARE-Bench基准测试，包含行为验证过的干净和恶意二进制文件；2）提出RARE-Guard框架，包含：Data-Only渲染（将二进制衍生内容仅显示为数据而非指令）、工具授权（Tool Authorization）、支持门（Support Gate，按来源单独计数支持证据）、来源门（Provenance Gate，按来源分组后再计数支持证据）、确定性渲染器（防止降级后的声明重新出现在最终报告中）。

Result: 无运行时控制时，模型在35/40个恶意案例中提出植入的不安全操作，0/40干净案例中未提出。Data-Only渲染下仍有15个不安全提议，工具授权拒绝了全部15个并授权了所有40个匹配的分析师请求。支持门验证了23/40虚假主张，来源门验证了0/40虚假主张并保留了所有40个受支持的主张。多工具融合实验中，支持门验证了32/32虚假主张，来源门防止了全部32个验证。

Conclusion: 二进制衍生内容可以在不获得指令权威的情况下指导分析，来自多个工具的观点不一定提供独立证据。RARE-Guard的授权和来源追踪机制能有效防止表征混淆攻击，确保分析系统不会将数据错误地提升为权威证据。

Abstract: LLM-assisted reverse-engineering (RE) systems analyze strings, decompiler output, and tool reports derived from ttacker-controlled binaries. A binary can make data look like instructions or records from one origin look like independent evidence. We call such failures Representation-Confusion Attacks in Reverse Engineering (RARE): the pipeline promotes a correctly extracted observation to instruction authority, claim-validating evidence, or trusted analysis state without the authority or support that role requires. RARE-Bench measures these failures with behavior-checked clean and adversarial binaries. After an exploratory 11,520-call study, we test RARE-Guard's authorization and evidence controls on 20 new programs and two models. Without runtime controls, the models propose a planted unsafe action in 35/40 adversarial cases and 0/40 clean cases. When binary-derived content is shown only as data (Data-Only rendering), they still make 15 unsafe proposals. Tool Authorization denies all 15 and authorizes all 40 matched analyst requests. On identical report drafts, Support Gate validates 23/40 false claims by counting records from one origin separately. Provenance Gate groups those records before counting support, validates 0/40 false claims, and retains all 40 supported claims. We then instrument Ghidra, r2pipe, and angr on 16 further programs. In a preselected eight-program subset, no single-tool draft reaches Support Gate's validation threshold for the false claim. In fused drafts across all 16 programs, Support Gate validates 32/32 false claims. Provenance Gate prevents validation of all 32 and retains all 32 supported claims. A deterministic renderer prevents downgraded claims from reappearing in the final report. Binary-derived content may therefore guide analysis without gaining authority over tools, and views from several tools do not necessarily provide independent evidence.

</details>


### [56] [Open-Source Intelligence and Music Information Retrieval for Geographic Attribution of Musical Affect and the Ecological Limits of Population Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12517)
*Mohammadreza Rashidi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文基于埃森民歌集分析了2393首民歌的旋律特征，发现不同国家间旋律结构存在显著差异（所有8个特征p<0.001），但旋律的情感特征与各国的幸福感和个人主义指数无显著相关（6个相关均不显著），反驳了“音乐反映民族性情”的直觉性观点，指出这是一种生态谬误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验“一个地区的音乐反映其人民性情”这一常见直觉——即忧郁的旋律标志忧郁的人群。作者希望测试这一直觉中可测度的部分，并拒绝其推断部分。

Method: 使用埃森民歌集中的2393首去重民歌（涵盖16个国家、7个地理区域），从符号乐谱中提取8种旋律特征（包括跳跃相关特征、活动度等），通过调性识别算法计算调式而非依赖文件标注；将区域音乐情感指标与《世界幸福报告》阶梯评分和霍夫斯泰德个人主义指数进行相关性分析。

Result: 各国间旋律结构差异显著（所有8个特征p<0.001，跳跃相关特征p<10^-90）；中国具有独特的宽跳跃、高活动度特征（唤醒度综合指标+1.24个标准差，平均音程间隔2.77个半音，德国为2.17）；6个音乐情感指标与国家幸福感/个人主义指数的相关均不显著（0/6）。

Conclusion: 音乐情感的地理分布是真实可测的，但它不能预测一个群体的幸福程度或个人主义程度；声称音乐情感能预测国民性格是一种生态谬误。研究发布了完整的提取与分析流程及可失败关闭的校验工具。

Abstract: A common intuition holds that a region's music mirrors the temperament of its people, so that melancholic melodies mark melancholic populations. We test the measurable half of that intuition and reject the inferential half. Using the Essen Folksong Collection, a corpus of thousands of notated folk melodies, we extract real melodic and affect-related features from 2393 deduplicated melodies spanning 16 countries and 7 geographic regions, with the analysis performed on symbolic scores rather than audio. The mode of each melody is computed with a key-finding algorithm rather than read from the file, because the collection's own documentation warns its major and minor labels are unreliable. Cross-country differences in melodic structure are large and highly significant. All 8 tested features differ across countries at p<0.001, with the leap-related features reaching p<10^-90, and China carries a distinctive wide-leap, high-activity signature (arousal composite +1.24 standard deviations, mean absolute interval 2.77 semitones against Germany's 2.17). We then test the inferential half. We correlate the regional musical-affect measures with two published, validated national indices, the World Happiness Report ladder score and the Hofstede individualism index. None of the 6 correlations is significant (0 of 6). The geography of musical affect is real and measurable, but it does not predict how happy or how individualist a population is, and any claim that it does is an ecological fallacy. We release the full extraction and analysis pipeline, and a fail-closed checker re-derives every number in this paper from the data.

</details>


### [57] [Open-Source Intelligence for Code Provenance and the Security Patterns that Separate Human and Large-Language-Model Implementations of Common Programming Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12524)
*Mohammadreza Rashidi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文构建了可完全复现的流水线，从9个语言模型和Stack Overflow收集31个安全敏感编程任务（如OAuth、JWT验证、密码哈希、SQL访问）的528个真实代码样本，通过确定性安全与风格检测器，训练分类器以93%准确率区分人类与模型代码来源（基线78%），7路分类器以48%准确率识别具体模型。结果显示安全模式在Python、JavaScript、Go、Java中均存在人类与模型的差异，且漏洞修复案例中模型有77%的修复率但存在“移除不安全模式却未添加正确防御”的失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究两个问题：(1) 代码片段能否被溯源到是人类编写还是大语言模型生成；(2) 两类来源在同一安全敏感编程任务中采用的安全模式是否存在差异。

Method: 构建完全可复现的流水线：从9个开源/开放权重语言模型和Stack Overflow API收集31个安全敏感编程任务（OAuth with PKCE、JWT验证、密码哈希、SQL访问等）的代码样本；使用确定性安全检测器和风格检测器对528个样本评分；训练交叉验证分类器进行二分类（人类vs模型）和7路分类（具体模型）；在Python、JavaScript、Go、Java四种语言上重复实验；进行漏洞修复案例研究（21个种子、12个弱点类别）。

Result: 人类vs模型二分类准确率93%（基线78%），7路模型归因准确率48%；安全模式差异在所有四种语言中均存在；溯源边界部分依赖语言且不对称迁移；漏洞修复率77%，但存在“移除了不安全模式却未添加正确防御”的常见失败模式。

Conclusion: 代码来源（人类vs模型）可从代码本身高精度恢复，两类来源在安全模式上存在系统性差异，且该差异跨语言成立；模型修复漏洞时存在不完全修复的固有问题；流水线完全可复现且易于扩展新任务和新语言。

Abstract: Developers now draw code from two very different sources, the accumulated human answers on sites such as Stack Overflow and the output of large language models. We ask two questions about that split. First, can the provenance of a code snippet be recovered from the code itself, and second, do the two sources differ in the security patterns they adopt for the same task. Using only open sources, a public gateway of open-weight language models and the public Stack Overflow API, we build a fully reproducible pipeline that collects real implementations of 31 security-sensitive programming tasks, among them OAuth with PKCE, JWT verification, password hashing, and SQL access, from 9 language models and from human answers, and scores every sample with deterministic security and style detectors. On 528 real samples we train a cross-validated classifier that recovers human versus model provenance with 93 percent accuracy against a 78 percent baseline, and a 7-way classifier that attributes a sample to the specific model that wrote it at 48 percent. We then report where the sources diverge on security, which patterns models adopt more often than the human corpus and which they inherit from it. Running the same tasks in Python, JavaScript, Go, and Java, we find the security divergence holds in every language while the provenance boundary is partly language-specific and does not transfer symmetrically between them. A vulnerability repair case study, in which models are handed insecure code and asked to fix it, finds a 77 percent repair rate across 21 seeds and 12 weakness classes, but a recurring partial-fix failure in which the model removes the insecure pattern without adding the correct defense. The pipeline is data driven, so any new task or language is added as a single specification entry, and a fail-closed checker re-derives every number in this paper from the stored data.

</details>


### [58] [VanillaBench: The Hidden Accuracy Cost of Adversarial Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12545)
*Niklas Bunzel*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: VanillaBench 系统性地量化了对抗训练模型与普通模型之间的准确率差距，发现所有186个鲁棒模型在干净数据上的准确率平均下降7.7-29.5个百分点，即使最强的鲁棒模型也落后其同期普通模型4.0-21.0个百分点，表明鲁棒性-准确率权衡比文献中通常呈现的要大得多。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对抗鲁棒性研究几乎普遍仅报告鲁棒模型自身的干净精度和对抗精度，但从未量化其与对应普通模型之间的准确率差距，导致决策者无法评估鲁棒性带来的实际准确率成本。

Method: 基于RobustBench中的186个对抗训练模型，从Papers with Code中获取多个普通参考模型，计算四种威胁模型下鲁棒模型与(1)所有普通模型条目、(2)无额外数据条目、(3)最强同期普通模型、(4)架构匹配基线之间的准确率差值，并进一步限制为精确架构匹配进行比较。

Result: 鲁棒模型与最强普通模型的平均干净准确率差距为-7.7到-29.5个百分点；单赛道最强鲁棒模型落后其同期普通模型4.0-21.0点；架构匹配对比显示平均差距为-3.5到-17.5点；精确架构匹配缩小至-4.0到-14.0点。

Conclusion: 对抗训练在干净准确率上的代价被严重低估，未来鲁棒性评估应将普通模型参考下的准确率差距作为标准报告项。

Abstract: Adversarial robustness research has produced hundreds of defended models over the past decade, yet the literature almost universally reports robustness results in isolation: standard (clean) accuracy and adversarial accuracy of the robust model are shown, but the gap to the corresponding vanilla model is rarely quantified. We introduce VanillaBench, a systematic benchmark that makes this gap explicit. For every adversarially-trained model catalogued by RobustBench across four threat models, we compute the accuracy difference against multiple vanilla references from Papers with Code, computed over both all entries and no-extra-data entries, the best vanilla model as of the robust model's publication year, and an architecture-matched baseline. Across all 186 robust models, the mean delta clean relative to the best vanilla model ranges from -7.7 to -29.5 percentage points, and even the single most robust model per track still trails its temporal vanilla counterpart by 4.0-21.0 points. The architecture-matched comparison, which isolates the effect of adversarial training from architectural differences, reveals a mean gap of -3.5 to -17.5 points. Restricting this architecture-matched comparison to models whose vanilla accuracy is known for the exact same architecture, rather than approximated from a related one, narrows the gap to -4.0 to -14.0 points. These results demonstrate that the robustness-accuracy trade-off is substantially larger than what is typically conveyed by individual papers. This information is critical for practitioners and decision-makers. When deploying models in real-world settings, the accuracy cost of robustness directly affects business outcomes, yet current publications do not provide the vanilla baseline needed to assess it. We argue that future robustness evaluations should report vanilla-referenced accuracy gaps as a standard component.

</details>


### [59] [How Agentic Is Agentic Commerce? A Population-Scale Measurement of x402 Adoption and Authenticity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12575)
*Shengchen Ling,Yajin Zhou,Lei Wu,Cong Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文揭示x402协议的数亿次结算并非AI经济采用的证明，而是可低成本人为制造的数据。通过对Base和Solana链上数据的测量，发现21.20%的交易是虚假的，63.78%为内部结算，真正独立支付仅占45.92%的价值，结算数量衡量的只是可制造性而非真实采用率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI代理经济中，x402协议的数亿次链上结算被广泛解读为AI经济已到来的证据。研究者怀疑这种计数可能被人为操纵，因此旨在揭示结算数量的真实含义，区分真实采用与人为制造。

Method: 对Base链进行280天的人口规模级测量，辅以Solana的粗粒度普查。通过链上事件识别结算，利用元交易层解析真实付款方，并构建支付图谱按交易痕迹的可证明程度对每笔交易进行分类。

Result: Base链上136,708,672次结算，价值$44,121,383.81，所有轴上的基尼系数均高于0.98（高度集中）。其中21.20%为虚假交易，63.78%为关联集群内部结算。真正独立且可证明到达可识别服务的仅$187,861.35，不可证明为人为制造的占$20,258,746.09（45.92%）。

Conclusion: x402的结算数量衡量的不是采用率，而是可制造性。所谓AI代理经济实际上是一个由运营商驱动、星形结构、机器定时且由Gas补贴的集中化系统，并非去中心化的自主经济。

Abstract: AI agents are said to be forming an economy in which they pay, on their own, for the data, APIs, and compute they consume. x402, which settles a stablecoin payment on-chain for each purchase, is the most widely deployed protocol for this, and its hundreds of millions of settlements are read as proof that the economy has arrived. We show the count cannot be read as adoption: it is the one metric an interested party can manufacture almost for free, since the facilitator sponsors the gas and nothing on-chain marks who controls a payment. We give the first population-scale measurement of x402 on Base, supplemented with a coarser Solana census. Identifying settlements from their on-chain event and resolving the true payer through the meta-transaction layer, we sort each by what its trace can prove via a payment graph. Over a 280-day window Base carries 136{,}708{,}672 settlements worth \$44{,}121{,}383.81, concentrated on every axis we measure (payer, recipient, and value Gini all above 0.98), yet 21.20\% are fictitious and 63.78\% internal settlement within a linked cluster. What is genuinely independent is bounded: it lies between the \$187{,}861.35 that demonstrably reaches a nameable service and the \$20{,}258{,}746.09 (45.92\% of value) not provably manufactured. Finally, we resolve the count's manufacturable component, a coherent operator-driven economy, star-shaped, machine-timed, and gas-subsidized. Settlement count measures manufacturability, not adoption.

</details>


### [60] [PVDetector: Detecting Prompt Injection Attacks on Purpose-Specific LLM Agents through Policy-Violation Concept Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12624)
*Junhui Wang,Hangtao Zhang,Zhirun Zheng,Li Zeng,Jiejun Xiao,Xi Luo,Lihua Yin,Saiqin Long*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出PVDetector，一种无需额外训练的框架，通过分析LLM隐藏激活空间中的潜在政策违规范念（PV概念）来检测提示注入攻击，在多个LLM和数据集上实现了低于1%的假阴性率，优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于输入-输出模式的提示注入检测方法效果有限，需要更有效的检测手段来防御针对特定目的智能体的攻击。

Method: PVDetector通过离线从政策违规与合规提示的对比对中提取PV概念，在推理时测量隐藏状态与PV概念的alignment（对齐程度），从而检测提示注入攻击。

Result: 在多个LLM和数据集上，PVDetector实现了低于1%的假阴性率，且辅助开销极小，一致优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: LLM内在的隐藏激活空间中存在反映政策违规语义的潜在概念，利用这一发现可以实现训练-free、高效且准确的提示注入检测。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed as purpose-specific agents to handle domain-specific tasks such as customer service and code generation. These agents are expected to comply with not only generic safety guardrails but also purpose-specific restrictions tailored to their designated roles. Such additional restrictions enlarge the attack surface, particularly to prompt injection (PI) attacks. To defend against such attacks, existing detection methods primarily rely on analyzing input-output patterns, yet yield limited effectiveness. To address this limitation, we turn to analyzing the hidden activation space and discover that LLMs inherently retain latent policy-violation (PV) concepts when prompted with requests beyond their designated purpose. Particularly, PV concepts capture the semantics of conflicts between user queries and predefined restrictions, implicitly reflecting LLMs' intrinsic awareness of recognizing policy violations. Building on this insight, we propose PVDetector, a training-free framework that detects PI attacks during LLM inference by measuring hidden-state alignment with PV concepts, which are derived offline from the contrastive pairs of policy-violating and policy-compliant prompts. Experiments across multiple LLMs and datasets show that PVDetector achieves <1\% false negative rate with minimal auxiliary overhead, consistently outperforming state-of-the-art methods. Our code is available at https://github.com/Claresigle/PVDetector .

</details>


### [61] [Auditable and Transparent Fully Authenticated Disk Encryption via USB Storage Interposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12716)
*Enrique Soriano-Salvador,Gorka Guardiola Múzquiz*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了CC（Cryptographic Companion）系统，一种基于通用单板计算机（如Raspberry Pi）的USB中间设备方案，用于实现全磁盘加密（FDE）。该系统位于主机和外部硬盘之间，透明地执行加密/解密和认证操作，为主机提供标准USB大容量存储接口，同时将加密数据存储在外部USB硬盘上。实验表明该原型系统在大多数常见用例中具有足够效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的全磁盘加密依赖操作系统驱动实现，存在对磁盘固件信任的潜在安全风险。作者探索一种替代方案：通过在主机和外部硬盘之间放置一个中间USB设备来执行加密操作，从而提供灵活性、低成本、透明性、使用通用硬件和开源软件，以及对恶意磁盘固件的缓解能力。

Method: 采用支持USB On-The-Go的通用单板计算机（如Raspberry Pi）作为中间设备，运行Linux系统。该设备提供标准USB大容量存储接口给主机，主机完全不知晓加密的存在；同时设备对外部USB硬盘执行加密/解密和认证操作。系统设计强调灵活性、低成本、透明性，并使用自由开源软件。

Result: 实现了CC原型系统，实验评估表明该方案在大多数常见使用场景中具有足够的效率。

Conclusion: 基于通用单板计算机的中间USB设备方案（CC）是一种可行的全磁盘加密替代方案，具有灵活性、低成本、透明性、通用硬件和开源软件等优势，并能缓解恶意磁盘固件的威胁，在常见用例下性能可接受。

Abstract: Full Disk Encryption (FDE) has become increasingly important in the last decades due to the evident confidentiality concerns. In most systems, encryption is provided by an operating system driver, through which the user can transparently access the encrypted disk after supplying the required keys (or the credentials from which those keys are derived). In this work, we explore an alternative approach: the use of an intermediate USB device placed between the host system and the external hard disk, where the encrypted data are stored. Although there are existing devices that follow this inline approach (such as RAID controllers and USB enclosures with built-in encryption), we explore the use of a general-purpose single-board computer running Linux with USB On-The-Go support (e.g. a Raspberry Pi), to provide FADE (Fully Authenticated Disk Encryption). Our system, named CC (Cryptographic Companion), performs the cryptographic operations (encryption/decryption and authentication), providing a standard USB mass-storage interface to the host (which is entirely unaware of the presence of encryption) while relying on the external USB hard disk to store the corresponding encrypted blocks. Our design provides several key advantages: flexibility, low cost, transparency, the use of generic hardware and free and open-source software, adaptability to emerging cryptographic schemes, and mitigations against malicious disk firmware. This paper presents the design and implementation of the CC and an experimental evaluation of our current research prototype, which indicates that it is sufficiently efficient for most common use cases.

</details>


### [62] [Bulkhead: Automated Semantic Detection and Remediation of Container Escape Vulnerabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12723)
*Qiyuan Fan,Zhi Li,Junjie Li,XiaoFeng Wang,Bin Yuan,Deqing Zou*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Bulkhead是一个利用大语言模型与形式化方法结合的自动化框架，用于发现和修复容器生态系统中因跨边界路径误解析导致的路径遍历漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 容器生态系统中文件系统隔离常因跨边界路径误解析而被削弱，导致路径遍历漏洞。现有防御手段存在缺陷：内核级保护侵入性强且未被Linux主线接受；检测方法依赖静态规则匹配或人工审计，前者无法捕获语义导致误报多，后者成本高且难以规模化。

Method: Bulkhead采用多智能体系统，通过多维知识模式识别和修复PaTra漏洞。检测流程先应用高风险功能模式定位跨边界交互入口，再用调用链模式恢复执行路径，分析调用链识别安全缺失和TOCTOU缺陷，并生成PoC验证。补丁流程通过断言驱动验证和模型检查模板确保修复正确性。

Result: 能够自动发现漏洞并生成PoC进行验证，同时通过断言驱动的形式化验证确保补丁正确性。

Conclusion: Bulkhead通过LLM与形式化方法的结合，为容器跨边界路径遍历漏洞提供了自动化的语义级检测与修复方案，克服了传统方法的局限性。

Abstract: Filesystem isolation in container ecosystems is often weakened by cross-boundary path misresolution, causing path traversal (PaTra) vulnerabilities. These vulnerabilities stem from insecure host-container interactions and have become increasingly pervasive as cloud systems mount shared resources, such as GPUs and agent workspaces, into containers to support AI workloads. Existing defenses remain inadequate. Kernel-level protections are intrusive, can destabilize system calls, and have therefore not been accepted into the Linux mainline. Detection methods rely on static rule matching or manual code auditing. Static rules can flag path-related functions but fail to capture the semantics needed to determine whether a host-container interaction exists, causing many false positives. Manual review requires domain expertise, making it costly, inefficient, and difficult to scale.
  To address this threat, we present Bulkhead, an automated framework that integrates large language models (LLMs) with formal methods for semantic vulnerability discovery and remediation. Bulkhead uses a multi-agent system to identify and repair PaTra vulnerabilities through multi-dimensional knowledge patterns generalized from known cases. It first applies high-risk functional patterns to locate entry points for cross-boundary interactions in containerized code, then uses call-chain patterns to recover the corresponding execution paths at suitable depth. The Detection pipeline analyzes these call chains against the application scenarios and threat model, identifying vulnerabilities such as missing security checks and TOCTOU flaws in cross-boundary interactions, and generating proof-of-concept (PoC) exploits for validation. These PoCs then guide patch generation. To ensure remediation correctness, the Patch pipeline performs assertion-driven verification using predefined model-checking templates.

</details>


### [63] [Stability Buys Time: A Re-Keying Game for Encrypted Multi-Agent Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12742)
*Sai Sandeep Damera,John S. Baras*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究在近似全同态加密（CKKS）控制回路中，针对高级持续性威胁（APT）的安全博弈模型，提出两阶段攻防博弈（被动侦察+主动操纵），证明重密钥（re-keying）而非自举（bootstrapping）是有效主动防御手段，并揭示FHE精度、控制精度与重密钥频率三者之间的权衡关系，存在一个"可安全化下限"与"静态足够上限"之间的有效安全窗口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近似FHE（CKKS）用于加密控制时，解密必然携带加密噪声（密钥泄露），而现有安全分析是静态的，未考虑反馈回路中持续解密的动态泄漏；同时现有假设多为诚实但好奇的云，缺乏对高级持续性威胁（APT）的建模。

Method: 将安全建模为两阶段博弈：（1）被动侦察阶段，简化为已知的洪泛权衡；（2）主动操纵阶段，建模为检测规避的时序博弈，采用Stackelberg均衡分析，防御者选择最懒惰的重密钥节奏来阻止对手，该节奏由图的拓扑脆弱性（控制理论中的边际稳定性）决定。

Result: 边际稳定图需要比重连接图更频繁地重密钥；存在FHE精度、控制精度和重密钥节奏的三方张力；定义了"可安全化下限"和"静态足够上限"之间的有效安全窗口。

Conclusion: 近似密码系统在反馈回路中的安全是一个动态博弈，防御者的行动是方案自身的刷新（重密钥），这一结论可推广到任何需要重复解密以执行动作的系统。

Abstract: Encrypted control lets a cloud coordinate a fleet of agents on fully homomorphically encrypted state, keeping their positions and commands private. The approximate scheme for real-valued control, CKKS, returns decryptions that carry the encryption noise, a key-recovery leak; the loop must decrypt to actuate, so the leak is unavoidable. Yet the security of approximate FHE is studied statically, encrypted control assumes an honest-but-curious cloud, and persistent-threat games never reach inside the cryptosystem. We model the loop's security under an advanced persistent threat as a two-phase game, passive reconnaissance then active manipulation, separated by a measured residual detector that sees only the manipulation. The passive phase reduces to the known flooding tradeoff; the active defense is re-keying, not bootstrapping, since only re-keying resets accumulated leakage. The active phase is a detection-evasion timing game: overt manipulation is caught, so the rational adversary stays stealthy, and at its Stackelberg equilibrium the defender re-keys on the laziest cadence that denies it, set by the control-theoretic fragility of the graph topology. The marginally-stable graph must re-key far more often than the well-connected one. A three-way tension among FHE precision, control accuracy, and re-key cadence sets where this game lives, between a securability floor and a static-suffices ceiling. The efficient secure point is that window, where re-keying is the price of precision efficiency. More broadly, security for an approximate cryptosystem in a feedback loop is a dynamic game whose defender's move is the scheme's own refresh, applying beyond control to any system that must repeatedly decrypt to act.

</details>


### [64] [A Scalable Cloud-Orchestrated and Service-Oriented Multi-Domain QKD Network with PQC Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12765)
*Konstantinos Krilakis,Antonia Tsili,Aikaterini Mandilara,Dimitris Syvridis*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一种融合供应商无关QKD、SDN编排和云管理信任服务的多域多站点量子安全网络架构，采用基于PQC的零信任网络访问协议，在真实多供应商测试环境中验证了可行性与性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有QKD网络因供应商特定接口、可信节点限制和互操作性差，难以跨异构基础设施和管理域扩展。

Method: 构建供应商无关的QKD、SDN编排与云管理信任服务集成的多域架构；基于零信任网络访问协议，采用PQC签名和密钥封装实现多级认证；部署包含3个供应商QKD节点及非QKD域的真实测试床。

Result: PQC和SDN开销在受限设备上较低，主要瓶颈是QKD密钥检索和供应商特定的密钥流限制。

Conclusion: 该框架在保持灵活性、互操作性和现有基础设施兼容性的同时，将量子安全密钥传输扩展到原生QKD边界之外。

Abstract: Quantum key distribution (QKD) offers unconditional security but existing QKD networks remain difficult to scale across heterogeneous infrastructures and administrative domains due to vendor-specific interfaces, trusted-node constraints, and limited interoperability. This work presents a flexible multi-domain and multi-site quantum-secure network architecture integrating vendor-agnostic QKD, SDN orchestration, and cloud-managed trust services. Communication is based on Zero Trust Network Access protocols featuring multi-level authentication mechanisms building upon post-quantum cryptography (PQC) signature and key encapsulation algorithms. The system is deployed on a real-world testbed with domains incorporating QKD nodes from 3 vendors, as well as domains without QKD infrastructure elements. Experimental results show that PQC and SDN overhead remain relatively low even on constrained devices, with the main bottleneck being QKD key retrieval and vendor-specific key streaming limitations. The proposed framework extends quantum-safe key transport beyond native QKD boundaries while preserving flexibility, interoperability, and compatibility with existing infrastructures.

</details>


### [65] [Silent Alarm: A J-Space Protocol for Comparing Danger Recognition Across Models and Quantization Levels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12792)
*Roman Prosvirnin,Victor Minchenkov,Alexey Soldatov,Vladimir Bashun*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出JADR协议，通过在模型生成首个响应token之前分析其Jacobian空间（J-space）中的内部表征来评估模型的越狱鲁棒性，无需外部评判模型，并引入SafetyAUC指标量化不同模型和量化/微调版本的安全机制强度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有越狱鲁棒性评估依赖LLM-as-judge对生成响应的评判，这种方法对评判流程敏感，且仅能观察到给定攻击集上的表面行为，无法揭示底层安全机制的隐藏脆弱性。

Method: JADR协议通过记录每个层中top-k的J-space tokens，将其归入六个行为情景轴，对比危险样本（StrongREJECT）和安全控制样本（XSTest/OKTest）之间的差异。计算全程在待评估模型的激活上本地完成，无需外部评判模型，最终使用SafetyAUC指标辅以bootstrap置信区间进行比较。

Result: 在六种模型（Qwen3系列四种变体、Gemma 2 9B）和三种权重量化精度（BF16/INT8/INT4）上应用JADR，并与StrongREJECT独立行为评估对照。SafetyAUC能够以统计显著性区分安全机制强/弱的模型，并捕捉到不同量化机制下显著不同的效应。

Conclusion: JADR提供了一种不依赖外部评判、基于模型内部表征的安全机制评估方法，能够揭示传统行为评估无法触及的隐藏脆弱性，且适用于跨模型和模型内修改（量化/微调）的公平比较。

Abstract: Jailbreak-robustness research typically evaluates safety through generated responses using an LLM-as-judge approach. Such evaluations, however, are sensitive to the benchmark's grading procedure and capture only observed behavior on a given set of attacks, without directly revealing the hidden fragility of the underlying safety mechanisms. This work proposes JADR (Jacobian Assessment of Danger Recognition), a protocol that measures a model's internal representation through Jacobian space (J-space, a recently proposed workspace of verbalizable concepts) before the first response token is generated. For every prompt and layer we record the top-k J-space tokens; these are grouped into six behavioral scenario axes and compared between a danger sample based on StrongREJECT and a safe control drawn from XSTest and OKTest. The method does not call on an external judge model: the computation runs entirely locally, on the activations of the model under evaluation, which lets us compare both different models against each other and modifications of a single model -- quantization and fine-tuning in particular -- on the same terms. The final comparison rests on the proposed SafetyAUC metric, complemented with bootstrap confidence intervals. The protocol is applied to six models (Qwen3-1.7B, Qwen3-4B, Qwen3-8B, Qwen3-Uncensored-4B, Qwen3-SafeRL-4B, Gemma 2 9B) across three weight-representation regimes -- BF16, INT8, and INT4 -- and checked against an independent behavioral evaluation with the StrongREJECT grader. The metric separates models with a strong versus a weak internal safety mechanism with statistical significance and captures substantively different effects across quantization regimes.

</details>


### [66] [Watermark Forensics for Generative Models: An Information-Theoretic Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13003)
*Xiaoyu Li,Zheng Gao,Xiaoyan Feng,Jiaojiao Jiang,Yulei Sui,Jiankun Hu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了生成模型水印的“法医阶梯”（检测→归属→提取→定位），并给出了每个层级在样本长度n上的理论代价。核心定理建立了多用户归属和载荷提取的熵率定律：对于统计无失真的方案，将文本归属到N个用户之一的代价为Θ(log N/h)个token，提取ℓ-bit载荷的代价为Θ(ℓ/h)，其中h为信源熵率。实验在GPT-2、Pythia-410M和Qwen2.5上验证了理论常数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有水印研究大多只关注“是否机器生成”的检测问题，但实际取证需要更丰富的功能（归属、提取、定位）。本文旨在构建一个统一的理论框架，系统回答不同取证层级在样本长度上的代价，并建立紧的熵率定律。

Method: 提出信息剖面ν(t)=I(S;X_t|X_{<t})统一刻画水印的信息承载特性；通过精确对齐（exact alignment）而非碰撞计数分析，设计基于实现困惑度（realized surprisal）阈值的解码器；推导检测、归属、提取、定位的样本复杂度上下界。

Result: 归属代价Θ(log N/h)，提取代价Θ(ℓ/h)，均为紧界（含(1+o(1))因子）。发现两个真实gap：Θ(log N)个token窗口内文本可证明是机器生成但无法归属；存在footprint-resolution不确定性原理。实验验证了理论常数。

Conclusion: 水印的取证能力存在层次化的样本复杂度代价，信息剖面是统一分析工具。归属和提取的熵率定律是紧的，且存在固有间隙而非建模伪影。

Abstract: A watermark in a generative model's output is usually asked only whether a text is machine-made. The same mark can do more: attribute it to the user who produced it, extract a hidden payload, or localize the part that survives editing. These form a forensic ladder, and we ask what each rung costs in the sample length $n$.
  One object organizes the answers. Let $S$ be the secret the mark carries (a user's identity or payload), and let the information profile $ν(t)=I(S;X_t\mid X_{<t})$ record how much the $t$-th token reveals about $S$ given the earlier ones. Its total mass pays for attribution and extraction; how that mass is spread pays for localization; and detection alone is paid for not by information but by presence, the distance from the marked to the unmarked distribution. The literature's two quality models, a mark subtle on every token and one that stamps a few tokens loudly, are two incomparable ways of capping this profile.
  Our main theorem settles the ladder's entropy column. For statistically distortion-free schemes, attributing a text to one of $N$ users costs $Θ(\log N/h)$ tokens over every stationary-ergodic source of entropy rate $h$, sharp to a $(1+o(1))$ factor: to our knowledge the first tight entropy-rate law for multi-user attribution (via exact alignment). The natural collision-counting analysis overcharges without bound; only a decoder thresholding each candidate by its own realized surprisal attains the rate while almost never implicating an innocent user. A matching converse makes the law two-sided, and extraction of an $\ell$-bit payload costs $Θ(\ell/h)$. Two gaps are real, not modeling artifacts: a $Θ(\log N)$-token window in which a text is provably machine-made yet unattributable, and a footprint-resolution uncertainty principle. Experiments on GPT-2, Pythia-410M, and Qwen2.5 recover the predicted constants.

</details>

### [67] [Optimal Adaptive Market Making: A Theoretical Framework for High-Yield Liquidity Provision in Perpetual Futures Markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11888)
*Minmin Zeng,Yi Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文为零手续费永续合约市场建立了严格的最优做市理论框架，包含PnL分解定理、HJB方程、高APY区域定理、主APY公式、跨交易所对冲策略、鲁棒性边界、Kelly最优杠杆及多资产组合分配等十个核心贡献，并通过数值分析揭示了盈利与亏损区间的相变。该框架统一并拓展了Avellaneda-Stoikov、Gueant-Lehalle-Fernandez-Tapia和Glosten-Milgrom等经典做市范式，适用于现代去中心化交易所微结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对去中心化永续合约市场中零手续费做市商缺乏严格理论框架的问题，旨在建立从随机最优控制出发的完整数学体系，统一经典做市模型以适应新型去中心化交易所的微结构特征。

Method: 采用滤波概率空间上的随机最优控制方法，将做市商的决策建模为自适应买卖价差与跨交易所库存对冲的联合控制问题；使用CARA效用函数推导HJB方程并给出验证定理；通过五个无量纲参数刻画高APY区域，推导主APY公式；结合资金费率动态分析最优进出阈值与对冲策略三分类；引入贝叶斯自适应估计、Kelly准则和多样化饱和结果进行多维度拓展。

Result: 推导出PnL分解定理（将收益拆分为价差收入、逆向选择损失、库存持有成本、对冲摩擦和资金费率暴露五个部分）；得到高APY区域的主公式；发现对冲策略的三分类（资金费率驱动下存在不同对冲区间）；给出指数级回撤概率上界和普适APY-VaR恒等式；得到最优控制下库存的遍历分布；推导出Kelly最优杠杆与破产边界；多资产组合存在多样化饱和效应。23张数值图展示了盈利与亏损区间的相变现象。

Conclusion: 该理论框架成功将传统做市模型（Avellaneda-Stoikov、Gueant-Lehalle-Fernandez-Tapia、Glosten-Milgrom）统一并拓展到去中心化永续合约市场，为零手续费环境下的做市商提供了从微观个体策略到宏观组合管理的完整理论指导，具有重要的实践和理论价值。

Abstract: We develop a rigorous theoretical framework for optimal market making in perpetual futures markets with zero maker fees. We model the market maker's problem as a stochastic optimal control problem on a filtered probability space, where the controls are adaptive bid-ask spreads and inventory hedging decisions across two exchanges. Our contributions include: (i) a PnL decomposition theorem separating revenue into spread income, adverse selection loss, inventory carrying cost, hedging friction, and funding rate exposure; (ii) the Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman equation for the joint spread-inventory-hedging control problem under CARA utility with a verification theorem; (iii) High-APY Regime Theorems characterizing profitable regions via five dimensionless parameters, culminating in a Master APY Formula; (iv) analysis of zero-fee economics on decentralized perpetual exchanges with optimal entry-exit thresholds; (v) optimal cross-exchange hedging policies with funding rate dynamics and a hedge regime trichotomy; (vi) a robustness margin quantifying parameter uncertainty tolerance; (vii) exponential drawdown probability bounds and a universal APY-VaR identity; (viii) ergodic inventory distribution under optimal control with Bayesian adaptive estimation; (ix) Kelly-optimal leverage with ruin boundaries; and (x) multi-pair portfolio allocation with diversification saturation results. Numerical analysis with twenty-three figures reveals phase transitions between profitable and unprofitable regimes. Our framework unifies and extends the Avellaneda-Stoikov, Gueant-Lehalle-Fernandez-Tapia, and Glosten-Milgrom paradigms for modern decentralized venue microstructure.

</details>


### [68] [In-Context Reinforcement Learning under Non-Stationarity: A Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11906)
*A Run,Ziluo Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文综述了非平稳环境下的上下文强化学习（ICRL）问题，重点探讨在部署策略参数固定不变的情况下，智能体如何仅通过上下文窗口内的交互历史来推断当前任务规则并适应环境变化，同时区分哪些历史经验仍然有效、哪些已过时。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ICRL综述主要围绕预训练目标、架构、上下文格式、评估协议和理论机制组织，而对非平稳设置（环境变化导致历史上下文失效）的关注不足。本文旨在填补这一空白，系统梳理非平稳ICRL的研究现状。

Method: 本文通过定义非平稳ICRL，将其与元强化学习、决策序列建模、检索增强强化学习、值/模型感知ICRL及奖励反馈智能体等方向关联，并沿着三个问题（什么在变化、变化如何展开、变化对智能体可观察性如何）组织文献综述。

Result: 提出了非平稳ICRL的核心挑战：在固定策略参数下，智能体必须同时推断当前决策规则和判断哪些历史证据仍支持该规则。将现有工作按变化类型、变化动态和可观察性三个维度进行了系统分类。

Conclusion: 非平稳ICRL是一个重要但尚未充分研究的方向，其核心在于智能体如何在变化环境中通过上下文实现"类学习"行为，而无需更新模型参数。

Abstract: The development of decision-pretrained transformers, algorithm distillation, long-context meta-RL, and retrieval-augmented agents has renewed interest in in-context reinforcement learning (ICRL): the ability of a pretrained or fine-tuned decision model to infer latent task rules and improve future behavior from interaction context, without test-time parameter updates. This line of work asks when trial-and-error evidence, rewards, transitions, demonstrations, feedback, or retrieved experience can make learning-like computation happen inside the context window. However, existing surveys of ICRL mainly organize the field around pretraining objectives, architectures, context formats, evaluation protocols, and theoretical mechanisms, while the non-stationary setting remains comparatively underexamined. In changing environments, accumulated context is not merely more evidence about a fixed task: the reward specification, transition kernel, observation channel, action interface, constraint model, or demonstration and memory distribution can fall out of alignment with the current regime. Previously useful context can therefore become stale, misleading, or useful again when an old regime returns. We survey non-stationary ICRL as the problem of adapting through context while deployed policy parameters remain fixed: the policy must infer both the current decision rule and which parts of its accumulated evidence still support that rule. We define non-stationary ICRL, relate it to meta-RL, decision sequence modeling, retrieval-augmented RL, value- and model-aware ICRL, and reward-feedback agents, and organize the literature along three questions: what changes, how the change unfolds, and how observable the change is to the agent.

</details>


### [69] [Ontology-Amplified Distillation and Contextuality Auditing for Sovereign Enterprise Language Models: A Combined Proof-of-Mechanism and Negative-Results Method Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11948)
*Thanh Luong Tuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文结合两项FAOS研究：1）基于Qwen3.6-27B的领域专用小模型蒸馏实验（在越南语金融任务上达到GPT-5基线水平，但统计功效不足）；2）企业Agent路由的语境性审计方法（结果显示无有效残留语境性）。两项研究均不支持部署、安全、优越性、统计等价性或语境性正路由规则的结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受数据居留法规约束的金融机构需要可在机构内部运行的租户自有语言模型，以避免数据出境风险。

Method: 1）本体增强蒸馏：使用前沿教师轨迹进行监督微调+基于本体的DPO训练Qwen3.6-27B学生模型，在单个Apple M5 Max上从47个合成跨领域偏好对训练；2）语境性审计方法：通过Contextuality-by-Default校正方法评估企业Agent路由中的分歧信号。

Result: 蒸馏模型在40个越南语金融任务中36个达到grounding（grounded率0.90，本体术语覆盖率0.95），与GPT-5基线持平但未超越；语境性审计中所有组别的校正上下文度均为零，有效信号为直接影响和构造耦合。

Conclusion: 证据不支持可部署性、安全性、优越性、统计等价性或基于语境性的正路由规则；本体蒸馏与语境性审计可配对作为模型构建与治理诊断的工具组合。

Abstract: Regulated financial institutions operating under data-residency rules need tenant-owned language models that can run inside the institution's perimeter. This paper combines two related FAOS studies into one mechanism-and-control article. First, it reports a reduced-power proof-of-mechanism study of ontology-amplified distillation: a Qwen3.6-27B student is adapted to the Foundation AgenticOS ontology through supervised fine-tuning on frontier-teacher trajectories and ontology-grounded direct preference optimization (DPO), trained locally on a single Apple M5 Max from 47 synthetic, English-language, cross-domain preference pairs.
